### [95] [Spin-incoherent Mott-Thouless pumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27321)
*Ajesh Kumar,Urban F. P. Seifert,Erez Berg,Achim Rosch*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一类"莫特-Thouless泵"，它能在具有大量熵的自旋非相干莫特态中实现指数级保护的量子化电荷输运，因为泵送电荷位于与热自旋自由度动态解耦的有间隙扇区中；同时识别出另一类缺乏此保护、自旋激发会快速破坏量子化输运的莫特-Thouless泵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的Thouless泵要求系统在整个泵浦过程中保持基态且热力学熵为零，这限制了其在实际高温或强关联系统中应用的可能性。作者希望探索在高度熵态（如莫特绝缘体中的自旋非相干态）下是否仍能保持量子化输运，从而拓展Thouless泵的概念适用范围。

Method: 作者在理论上确定了莫特-Thouless泵的类别，通过解析论证展示泵送电荷所在的有间隙扇区与热自旋自由度之间的动态解耦机制，从而保证输运的量子化。同时，基于近期实验实现，识别出缺乏这种保护的泵浦类别。最后通过有限系统的数值精确实时模拟验证了解析结果。

Result: 提出了一类Mott-Thouless泵，它们在具有惊人熵值的自旋非相干莫特态中仍能表现出指数级保护的量子化输运；相反，另一类莫特-Thouless泵由于自旋激发能高效产生电荷激发，导致量子化输运迅速失效。数值模拟支持这些解析结论。

Conclusion: 量子化的电荷泵浦并不一定要求系统处于零熵的基态；在某些莫特系统中，即使存在大量自旋熵，只要泵送的电荷自由度与热自旋自由度保持动态解耦，量子化输运就能被指数级保护。这为高熵态下的拓扑输运研究开辟了新方向，并为区分受保护与非受保护的莫特-Thouless泵提供了理论判据。

Abstract: A Thouless pump describes a system in which a quantized amount of charge is transported by one lattice spacing per cycle when the parameters of a Hamiltonian are varied slowly and periodically. In the standard case, this quantization requires the system to remain in its ground state with vanishing thermodynamic entropy throughout the pumping process. Here, we introduce a class of Mott-Thouless pumps, which operate in highly entropic, spin-incoherent Mott states. We show analytically that these states exhibit exponentially protected quantized transport despite their extensive entropy, because the pumped charge resides in a gapped sector that remains dynamically decoupled from the hot spin degrees of freedom. By contrast, motivated by a recent experimental realization, we identify other classes of Mott-Thouless pumps that lack this protection. In these systems, spin excitations can efficiently generate charge excitations, leading to a rapid breakdown of quantized transport. Our analytical results are supported by numerically exact real-time simulations of finite systems.

</details>


### [96] [Altermagnetism from a Cu-Fe Lieb Lattice in FeSe/Cuprate Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27331)
*Ying Li,Augustin Davignon,Peng Rao,Runhan Li,Maia G. Vergniory,Roser Valentí,Johannes Knolle*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出FeSe/铜氧化物超导异质结可作为实现交错磁（altermagnetism）的平台，通过45°扭转Cu/Fe层形成CuFe₂ Lieb晶格产生d波自旋劈裂，或通过衬底诱导Se位不等价性将Fe层中的交错磁转移到铜氧化物层，并利用DFT计算验证了两种机制的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高T_c铜氧化物体系中实现交错磁可为研究无净磁化条件下的自旋劈裂电子能带以及其与非常规超导的相互作用提供直接途径，但目前缺乏这样的平台。

Method: 提出两种机制：(1) FeSe/铜氧化物异质结中Cu与Fe层45°扭转形成CuFe₂ Lieb晶格，Fe磁序与Cu-Fe杂化共同诱导d波交错磁自旋劈裂，用最小紧束缚模型证明其普适性；(2) 衬底诱导FeSe中两个Se位不等价，使交错磁起源于Fe层并通过近邻效应转移到铜氧化物层。通过FeSe/Bi₂Sr₂CuO₆异质结的DFT计算验证两种机制。

Result: DFT计算证实了两种交错磁产生机制在FeSe/Bi₂Sr₂CuO₆异质结中的可行性，并揭示了增强自旋劈裂的途径。最小紧束缚模型表明第一种机制具有普适性。

Conclusion: 超导铜氧化物/过渡金属硫族化物异质结为工程化交错磁以及研究其与非常规超导耦合提供了有前景的平台。

Abstract: Realizing altermagnetism in high-$T_c$ cuprate-based systems would provide a direct route for studying spin-split electronic bands in the absence of net magnetization and investigate their interplay with unconventional superconductivity. Here, we propose that FeSe/cuprate heterostructures offer such a platform, where a 45$^\circ$ twist of Cu and Fe layers creates an effective CuFe$_2$ Lieb lattice in which Fe magnetic order and Cu-Fe hybridization through the ligands induces altermagnetic $d$-wave spin splitting. A minimal tight-binding model shows that this mechanism is generic. Furthermore, a substrate-induced inequivalence of the two Se sites in FeSe provides a second route in which altermagnetism originates in the Fe layer and is transferred to the cuprate layer by proximity. Density functional theory calculations for FeSe/Bi$_2$Sr$_2$CuO$_6$ heterostructures confirm the viability of both mechanisms and reveal ways to enhance the spin splitting. These results establish superconducting cuprate/transition metal chalcogenide heterostructures as a promising setting for engineering altermagnetism and studying its coupling to unconventional superconductivity.

</details>


### [97] [Flat-band formation and chiral superconductivity in driven topological insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27355)
*Suman Jyoti De,Leo Goutte,Kartiek Agarwal,T. Pereg-Barnea*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文展示了利用圆偏振光对三维拓扑绝缘体表面进行Floquet工程调控，可得到近平坦带或墨西哥帽型电子能带，在纯排斥库仑相互作用下支持拓扑超导。使用典型3D TI参数，平坦带极限在合理电场下即可实现，通过调节光场强度获得的能带与菱面体石墨烯在位移场下的能带相似。在合适筛选金属栅极辅助下，该体系可在低电子密度范围（10^11-10^12/cm²）获得约7K的超导转变温度，同时避免维格纳结晶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用光场驱动（Floquet工程）在三维拓扑绝缘体表面实现平坦带或墨西哥帽型能带，从而在纯排斥库仑相互作用下诱导拓扑超导，为该类体系提供了新的量子调控途径，并可与近期菱面体石墨烯中纯排斥相互作用超导的方案相类比。

Method: 利用圆偏振光对3D TI表面态进行Floquet工程，通过调节光场强度改变表面态色散的固有曲率（可减弱甚至翻转符号），从而构造近平坦带或墨西哥帽型能带；结合筛选金属栅极调控库仑相互作用，评估排斥作用下超导配对的可能性及转变温度。

Result: 平坦带极限在合理电场参数下可达；驱动场强度变化所实现的能带在能量和空间分布上与菱面体石墨烯在变化位移场下的能带类似；在合适放置的筛选金属栅极辅助下，可在10^11-10^12/cm²的电子密度范围内获得约Tc≈7K的超导转变温度，且避免维格纳结晶。

Conclusion: 圆偏振光Floquet工程是在3D TI表面实现平坦带/墨西哥帽带并进一步驱动拓扑超导的有效手段；通过调节光场强度和引入筛选栅极，可在实验中可行的参数范围内实现约7K的超导转变温度，为基于光场调控的拓扑超导器件提供了前景。

Abstract: We demonstrate that circularly polarized light can be used to Floquet-engineer nearly flat or Mexican-hat like electronic bands on the surface of three-dimensional topological insulators (3D TIs), which under suitable conditions, can support topological superconductivity via purely repulsive Coulomb interactions. The driving acts not merely by gapping out the Dirac cone on the surface of the 3D TI, but can be used to diminish, and even flip in sign, the intrinsic curvature of the surface state dispersion away from the Dirac point. Using parameters for canonical 3D TIs, we find that the flat band limit is attained for reasonable electric fields and the bands realized by changing the strength of the driving field have a similar energetic and spatial profile to those obtained in rhombohedral graphene under varying displacement field, where the case for superconductivity with purely repulsive interactions has recently been made. We find that, with the aid of appropriately placed screening metallic gate, one can obtain $T_c \sim 7 $K in this setup while avoiding Wigner crystallization for low electron densities in the range of $10^{11}-10^{12}/\text{cm}^2$.

</details>


### [98] [Excited state optimization for strongly correlated quantum defects using ensemble variational Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27377)
*Kevin G. Kleiner,Lucas K. Wagner*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究利用集成变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）方法，对强关联点缺陷（包括金刚石中的氮空位和硅空位中心，以及氮化铝中的替代铁和铬杂质）的优化波函数进行了系统研究，发现完全优化行列式展开参数、轨道和Jastrow关联因子对激发能计算至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强关联点缺陷（如色心）在量子技术中具有重要应用，但准确计算其激发态性质充满挑战。传统方法在描述强关联电子时存在局限，需要更精确的波函数优化方法来获得可靠的激发能。

Method: 采用新引入的集成变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）方法，对四种强关联点缺陷体系进行波函数优化。系统研究了三个参数的优化效果：(1) 行列式展开参数、(2) 轨道（比较PBE0杂化泛函与PBE半局域泛函）、(3) Jastrow关联因子。通过在真实空间直接优化目标泛函来评估各参数对激发能的贡献。

Result: 研究发现：PBE0杂化泛函轨道显著优于半局域PBE轨道，导致激发能变化最高达0.5 eV；直接优化目标泛函可进一步改进，使激发能变化最高达0.2 eV。不同缺陷中起主导作用的优化参数各不相同，表明所有参数都需要同时优化才能获得精确的激发态。

Conclusion: 研究证实了在强关联点缺陷中，全面优化所有波函数参数（行列式系数、轨道、Jastrow因子）对获得准确激发态是必要的。PBE0轨道作为初始猜测具有显著优势，但直接优化目标泛函仍能带来额外改进。该工作为VMC方法在强关联缺陷体系中的应用提供了重要基准。

Abstract: Using the recently introduced ensemble variational Monte Carlo (VMC), we study optimized wave functions for strongly correlated point defects, including nitrogen-vacancy and silicon-vacancy centers in diamond and substitutional iron and chromium impurities in aluminum nitride. We study the effects of fully optimized determinant expansion parameters, orbitals, and Jastrow correlation factors on these systems. We find that orbitals from the hybrid functional PBE0 are much better (have lower objective functional) than semilocal PBE, which results in changes in the excitation energies up to 0.5 eV. Further improvements can be made by directly optimizing the objective functional, resulting in changes in excitation energies up to 0.2 eV. The most important parameter varies from defect to defect, reinforcing the necessity of optimizing all parameters to obtain accurate excited states in strongly correlated defects.

</details>


### [99] [Universality of Energy-Space Entanglement in Quantum Impurity Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27394)
*Geng-Dong Zhou,Zhi-Da Song*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子杂质模型中能量空间二分的纠缠熵（EE），发现其在高对称性下表现出普适行为。对于具有费米液体不动点的模型（如Anderson模型、Kondo模型），低能EE流到仅依赖于对数离散化参数Λ的整数倍ln2常数，其起源可归因于一维拓扑边缘模。在非费米液体临界点，EE表现出不稳定的平台，其Λ依赖性与过屏蔽两通道Kondo模型类似，支持同一普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠熵研究多基于实空间二分，但作者希望探索能量空间（等价于浴的动量空间）二分的纠缠熵，以揭示量子杂质模型中新的普适结构，并通过拓扑带理论对固定点进行分类。

Method: 受poor man's scaling启发，作者对浴进行对数离散化，将其划分为高能与低能两个区域；将能量空间固定点波函数的尺度不变性映射为一维链上的有效平移不变性，利用一维拓扑带理论对固定点进行分类；并通过两轨道Anderson模型中的局部单态-近藤单态转变研究不同费米液体固定点之间的跃迁。

Result: 费米液体固定点的低能EE流向与模型参数无关的常数，为ln2的整数倍加上仅依赖对数离散化参数Λ的修正；在具有低能手征对称性时，每个ln2贡献来自一个拓扑边缘模。局部单态相EE几乎为零，而近藤单态相每自旋每轨道EE大于ln2；在手征对称性成立时，这一区别对应有效浴链的拓扑转变。非费米液体临界点的EE显示出不稳定平台，其Λ依赖性与过屏蔽两通道Kondo模型相似。

Conclusion: 能量空间纠缠熵为量子杂质模型的固定点分类提供了新的普适工具，将费米液体固定点与一维拓扑带理论、ln2量子化的拓扑边缘模联系起来，并支持非费米液体普适类的统一描述。

Abstract: Entanglement entropy (EE) is commonly studied using real-space bipartitions. We show that, in quantum impurity models, an energy-space bipartition, equivalent to the momentum-space bipartition of the bath, can display universal behavior. Motivated by poor man's scaling, we logarithmically discretize the bath and partition it into high- and low-energy sectors. For models with Fermi-liquid fixed points, including the Anderson model and fully screened or underscreened Kondo models, the low-energy EE flows to constants independent of model parameters. These constants are integer multiples of $\ln 2$ plus corrections that depend only on the logarithmic discretization parameter $Λ$. We show that scale invariance of the fixed-point wavefunction in energy space maps to effective translation invariance along a one-dimensional chain, allowing the fixed points to be classified by one-dimensional topological band theory. With low-energy chiral symmetry, each $\ln 2$ contribution originates from a topological edge mode. We also study transitions between distinct Fermi-liquid fixed points using the local-singlet--Kondo-singlet transition in a two-orbital Anderson model driven by an inter-orbital antiferromagnetic coupling. The local-singlet phase has an effectively decoupled impurity and nearly vanishing EE, whereas the Kondo-singlet phase has finite EE larger than $\ln 2$ per spin and orbital. When chiral symmetry holds at low energies, this distinction corresponds to a topological transition of the effective bath chain. At the non-Fermi-liquid critical point, the EE develops an unstable plateau. Its $Λ$ dependence resembles that of the overscreened two-channel Kondo model, supporting universality within the same non-Fermi-liquid universality class.

</details>


### [100] [Iterative minimization in reduced density matrix functional theory for periodic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27679)
*Kai Luo,Jingang Han,Peize Lin,Daye Zheng,Mohan Chen,Xinguo Ren*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基组无关的约化密度矩阵泛函理论（RDMFT）框架，并实现了基于平面波基组的周期性固体计算方案，采用自适应压缩交换机制评估非局域交换相关泛函，利用谱投影梯度（SPG）方法优化自然占据数并通过黎曼优化更新自然轨道。在H2、硅、钠等体系上的基准测试表明SPG优于显式-隐式（EBI）映射方法，并在分数电荷LiH、H2和N2解离以及硅的状态方程等应用中验证了算法的可靠性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RDMFT为强关联体系提供了一条超越Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论的途径，但对于周期性固体的实际计算仍然难以实现。作者旨在突破这一瓶颈，将RDMFT推广到扩展体系并实现可用的平面波数值方案。

Method: 作者以基组无关的方式表述RDMFT，并利用平面波基组实现周期性固体的迭代最小化方案。非局域交换相关泛函通过现有的自适应压缩交换机制求值。自然占据数在N-可表示性约束下用谱投影梯度法（SPG）或一阶显式-隐式（EBI）映射优化，自然轨道则通过复Stiefel流形上的黎曼优化更新。

Result: 在HF泛函下对H2、硅和钠的基准测试显示SPG可以复现收敛的混合泛函参考结果，而EBI在占据数接近0或1时可能停滞。使用Power和Müller泛函时，SPG比EBI获得更低的能量和更稳定的收敛。在分数电荷LiH、H2和N2解离以及硅状态方程等应用中验证了算法的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该平面波RDMFT实现是可靠且鲁棒的，为强关联周期性固体的RDMFT实用计算迈出了重要一步，SPG方法为推荐的能量优化策略。

Abstract: Reduced density matrix functional theory (RDMFT) offers a route beyond Kohn-Sham density functional theory for strongly correlated systems, yet practical calculations for periodic solids are still out of reach. We formulate RDMFT for extended systems in a basis-independent way and present a planewave implementation using iterative minimization for periodic solids, evaluating nonlocal exchange-correlation functionals through the existing adaptive compressed exchange machinery. Natural occupations are optimized under N-representability constraints with a spectral projected gradient (SPG) method or an first-order explicit-by-implicit (EBI) map, while natural orbitals are updated by Riemannian optimization on the complex Stiefel manifolds. Benchmarks on typical systems of \ce{H2}, silicon, and sodium with the Hartree-Fock functional show that SPG reproduces converged hybrid references, whereas EBI can stall when occupations approach $0$ or $1$. With the power and Müller functionals, SPG yields lower energies and more stable convergence than EBI. Applications to fractionally charged \ce{LiH}, dissociating \ce{H2} and \ce{N2} molecules, and equation of state of silicon show that the algorithm presented in this implementation is reliable and robust.

</details>


### [101] [Trion Excitations in Twisted Bilayer Graphene: A Quantum Monte Carlo Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27745)
*Shibo Shan,Cheng Huang,Patrick Ledwith,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过连续场动量空间量子蒙特卡洛方法，揭示了扭曲双层石墨烯有限温度正常态中奇异的"狄拉克三子"（Dirac trion）激发。这些无间隙的三粒子束缚态（两个电子和一个空穴）在零动量点与高能电子严格正交，具有"极轻"的特性，可通过扭转角度和层间跳跃强度进行调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定强关联电子系统中电荷载体的本质是基本目标。作者希望超越朗道能级之外的框架，在投影相关平带系统中探索新的多体激发，理解扭曲双层石墨烯中有限温度正常态下的奇异激发机制。

Method: 采用连续场动量空间量子蒙特卡洛（continuous-field momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo）无偏量子多体计算方法，在有限温度下研究扭曲双层石墨烯的对称正常态，分析其激发谱和三粒子束缚态结构。

Result: 发现基态为具有约20 meV能隙的对称破缺绝缘体，但仅约3 meV的小温度（远低于相互作用尺度）即可驱动系统进入强涨落的对称正常态。该正常态中存在由两个电子和一个空穴组成的无间隙三粒子束缚态——狄拉克三子，其在零动量无间隙点与高能电子严格正交，且虽然由重载流子组成却任意轻，其谱可通过扭转角和层间跳跃强度调控。

Conclusion: 狄拉克三子的发现为投影相关平带系统中新型多体激发的理解提供了基础，打开了对强关联拓扑带中超越朗道能级的多体激发做进一步研究的大门。

Abstract: Determining the nature of charge carriers is a fundamental goal in the study of strongly correlated electron systems. Here, we employ the continuous-field momentum-space quantum Monte Carlo method to reveal exotic "Dirac trion" excitations in the finite-temperature normal state of twisted bilayer graphene. While the ground state is a symmetry-breaking insulator with gapped ($\sim$ 20 meV) electron-like excitations, we show that a small temperature ($\sim$ 3 meV), well below the interaction scale, drives the system into a strongly fluctuating symmetric normal state. We demonstrate that this normal state hosts gapless excitations consisting of three-particle bound states, two electrons and one hole, that are exactly orthogonal to the higher-energy electrons at the zero-momentum gapless point. These Dirac trions have the remarkable property of being arbitrarily light despite being composed of heavy constituents, and their spectra can be easily tuned by varying the twist angle and interlayer hopping strength. Our unbiased quantum many-body computation sheds light on the Dirac trions in a projected correlated flat-band setting and opens the door for further investigation of many-body excitations in strongly correlated topological bands beyond Landau levels.

</details>


### [102] [Finite-size effects and interaction-driven crossovers in quarter-filled attractive Hubbard model: Exact diagonalization, DMRG and machine-learning analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27916)
*Md Fahad Equbal,Satoru Hayami*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用精确对角化、DMRG以及无监督机器学习方法研究有限宽圆柱格子上的四分之一填充吸引性Hubbard模型，揭示了从弱关联费米子到紧密束缚单态对的连续相互作用驱动的交叉转变，该交叉与BCS-BEC转变一致，并通过机器学习方法（PCA和UMAP）以不依赖序参量的方式独立确认了相同的交叉区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在获得对吸引性Hubbard模型中相互作用驱动的交叉转变的无偏表征，特别是探索如何利用无监督机器学习方法（PCA和UMAP）来识别和刻画从弱配对到强配对（BCS-BEC交叉）的物理区域，而不依赖传统的序参量。

Method: 采用精确对角化和密度矩阵重整化群方法计算基态能量、局域观测量和相关函数；通过空穴束缚能计算提供配对形成的直接能量证据；应用主成分分析和均匀流形近似投影对实空间相关矩阵进行无监督机器学习分析，并结合有限尺寸标度分析。

Result: 发现连续相互作用驱动的交叉从弱关联费米子过渡到紧密束缚单态对支配的区域，行为与BCS-BEC交叉一致。双空穴束缚能在整个吸引区保持负值，而三空穴束缚能仅在足够强吸引下出现并呈现明显有限尺寸依赖性。PCA和UMAP独立识别出与常规观测量一致的交叉区域，且该信号在有限尺寸标度下保持稳健。

Conclusion: 无监督机器学习方法（PCA和UMAP）能够以不依赖序参量的方式提供多体关联重组的无偏表征，并独立确认了与BCS-BEC交叉一致的物理交叉区域，为强关联体系中的交叉转变研究提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: We investigate the quarter-filled attractive Hubbard model on finite-width cylindrical lattices using exact diagonalization (ED), density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and unsupervised machine-learning-based techniques. Analysis of the ground-state energetics, local observables and correlation functions reveals a continuous interaction-driven crossover from weakly correlated fermions to a regime dominated by tightly bound singlet pairs. This crossover originates from the competition between kinetic-energy-driven fermionic itinerancy and interaction-driven onsite pair formation and exhibits behavior consistent with the BCS--BEC crossover in the thermodynamic limit. Hole-binding-energy calculations provide direct energetic evidence for pair formation: the two-hole binding energy remains negative throughout the attractive regime whereas three-hole binding emerges only at sufficiently strong attraction and exhibits pronounced finite-size dependence. To obtain an unbiased characterization of the correlation landscape, we apply principal component analysis (PCA) and uniform manifold approximation and projection (UMAP) to the real-space correlation matrices. PCA reveals a systematic redistribution of correlation variance whereas UMAP identifies a clear separation between weak- and strong-pairing regimes. Both machine-learning-based approaches independently identify the same crossover region inferred from conventional observables while providing an order-parameter-independent characterization of the underlying reorganization of many-body correlations. Finite-size scaling analyses of the pairing structure factor and the leading PCA variance ratio demonstrate that these signatures remain robust with increasing system size.

</details>


### [103] [Orbital-Selective Mott Transition and Correlation-Amplified Charge Ordering in the Altermagnet CsCr$_2$S$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28029)
*Xiuhua Chen,Yilin Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究通过DFT+DMFT计算揭示了CsCr₂S₂O中由配体（S位）畸变和条纹电荷序驱动的Verwey型金属-绝缘体转变（MIT）的本质：轨道选择性的Mott转变负责低能物理，S位畸变通过Cr-d_yz与S-p轨道杂化引发微小的电荷不对称，而动态电子关联显著放大这种不对称性，导致不同Cr位点在电荷、电子关联及局域自旋极化上出现巨大差异，最终驱动MIT。置换S为Te会削弱这种关联放大效应而无法实现MIT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CsCr₂S₂O作为铝磁体（altermagnet），其Verwey型MIT由配体位点（S位）的晶格畸变驱动，而非Cr位点本身，这导致Cr位点间电荷失衡的物理机制尚不明确。理解这一谜团对于揭示铝磁体中配体工程的重要性以及实现稳健的金属态铝磁体具有关键意义。

Method: 采用DFT+DMFT（密度泛函理论+动力学平均场理论）计算方法，系统分析了CsCr₂S₂O在Verwey转变前后的电子结构。通过考察Cr-d_yz轨道与S-p轨道的杂化效应、动态电子关联对电荷不对称性的放大机制，以及S被Te替代后关联变化的对比研究，揭示了MIT的微观驱动机制。

Result: 发现MIT本质上是轨道选择性Mott转变，低能物理由强关联的d_yz金属轨道主导。S位畸变通过Cr-d_yz和S-p杂化产生初始微小电荷不对称，该不对称被动态电子关联大幅放大，导致不同Cr位点间电荷量和电子关联程度出现显著差异，进而引发局域自旋极化的分化并驱动MIT。对比研究表明，以Te替代S会削弱关联放大效应，无法诱导MIT。

Conclusion: 多体效应能够大幅放大配体不稳定性从而重塑铝磁体的电子结构，配体工程对于实现稳健的金属态铝磁体至关重要。该研究为理解铝磁体中MIT的微观机制提供了新的视角，并为设计新型金属态铝磁材料指出了配体调控的方向。

Abstract: Altermagnet CsCr$_2$S$_2$O undergoes a Verwey-type metal-to-insulator transition (MIT) driven by lattice distortion and a stripe charge order on the Cr sublattice, reminiscent of the physics in Fe$_3$O$_4$. However, atomic distortions occur exclusively at the ligand sites rather than the Cr sites. What drives such a pronounced charge imbalance between Cr sites thus remains a mystery. Utilizing DFT+DMFT calculations, we identify an orbital-selective Mott transition, which leaves the correlated metallic $d_{yz}$ orbital governing the low-energy physics. We demonstrate that S-site distortions trigger an initial, tiny charge asymmetry between Cr sites via Cr-$d_{yz}$ and S-$p$ orbital hybridization. Crucially, this asymmetry is significantly amplified by dynamical electronic correlations, resulting in a large discrepancy in both the charge and electron correlations of the Cr-$d_{yz}$ orbital between distinct Cr sites. This further induces a substantial differentiation in local spin polarizations in the altermagnetic state, ultimately driving the MIT. In contrast, we predict that replacing S with Te weakens this correlation-amplification effect and fails to induce an MIT due to weaker electron correlations. Our findings demonstrate that many-body effects can drastically amplify ligand instabilities to reshape the electronic structure of altermagnets, highlighting that ligand engineering is of paramount importance for realizing robust metallic altermagnetism.

</details>


### [104] [Thermal spin transport in easy-planar $d$-wave altermagnets controlled by magnetic field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28085)
*Yuliia I. Gusieva,Kostiantyn V. Yershov,Jeroen van den Brink,Volodymyr P. Kravchuk*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了金红石型易面d波交变磁体（如NiF2）中交变磁致磁振子磁矩的产生及其引发的自旋劈裂器效应，并展示了该效应可通过垂直于易面的外磁场有效调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交变磁体是一类新型共线自旋补偿材料，其磁振子分支即使在非相对论极限下也存在自旋劈裂，这一性质强烈影响磁振子输运性质，导致易轴d波交变磁体中出现热磁振子劈裂器效应。然而该效应是否在易面系统中出现尚不明确，因为此时磁振子分支不携带恒定磁矩。因此需要研究易面d波交变磁体中的相关效应。

Method: 以金红石型易面d波交变磁体（如NiF2）为研究对象，通过理论分析研究其磁振子性质，证明交变磁致磁振子磁矩的出现——该磁矩具有动量依赖性和d波对称性，并由此推导磁振子驱动的自旋电流（磁矩流）在温度梯度下的产生。

Result: 证明了在易面d波交变磁体中存在交变磁致磁振子磁矩，该磁矩与动量相关且具有d波对称性，进而导致自旋劈裂器效应——即在施加温度梯度时产生磁振子驱动的自旋电流（磁矩流）。此外，对应的热自旋电导率可通过垂直于易面的外磁场有效调控。

Conclusion: 易面d波交变磁体同样能够产生交变磁相关的自旋分裂效应，且其热自旋输运性质可通过外磁场灵活调控，这为交变磁体在自旋电子学领域的应用提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Altermagnets constitute a novel class of collinear spin-compensated materials in which magnon branches are spin-split even in the non-relativistic limit. The latter is the result of a more complex symmetry operation (compared to conventional antiferromagnets) that connects the two sublattices. Altermagnetic splitting strongly affects the magnon transport properties, leading, in particular, to the thermal magnon splitter effect in easy-axial $d$-wave altermagnets. Whether this effect also appears in easy-planar systems is not obvious, since the magnon branches do not carry a constant magnetic moment in this case. Here, we consider the easy-planar $d$-wave altermagnet of a rutile-type, e.g., NiF$_2$, and demonstrate the emergence of the altermagnetically generated magnon magnetic moment, which is momentum-dependent and possesses $d$-wave symmetry. This then leads to the spin-splitter effect, i.e., the emergence of a magnon-driven spin current (the flow of magnetic moment) in response to the applied temperature gradient. We also demonstrate that the corresponding thermal spin conductivity can be effectively tuned by an external magnetic field perpendicular to the easy plane.

</details>


### [105] [Coupled Spin-Density-Wave and Bond-Order Driven Metal-Insulator Transition in Altermagnetic CsCr$_2$S$_2$O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28329)
*Chenchao Xu,Wansheng Bai,Guo-Xiang Zhi,Yi Liu,Xiaoqun Wang,Jianhui Dai,Chao Cao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算揭示了CsCr₂S₂O中由键序(BO)与次级自旋密度波(sSDW)耦合驱动的金属-绝缘体转变(MIT)，其机制依赖于预先存在的C型反铁磁序破坏时间反演对称性，以及Cr-d_yz和Cr-d_xz轨道选择性分化的物理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解CsCr₂S₂O中金属-绝缘体转变的结构起源和微观机制，特别是键序如何与自旋密度波耦合，以及预先存在的磁性序如何促成这种耦合。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算，分析Cr-3d轨道的轨道选择性行为：d_yz轨道形成局域磁矩并建立交变磁(alternagnetic)序，d_xz轨道保持金属性并与S-p_z轨道杂化。在此基础上研究在位库仑相互作用如何增强巡游d_xz电子的次级自旋密度波不稳定性，并分析该不稳定性与d_xz-S-p_z键序的耦合。

Result: 识别出sSDW-BO耦合态，该耦合态同时产生实验观测到的结构畸变、电荷不均化、局部Cr磁矩调制和带隙打开，从而完整解释了实验中观察到的结构MIT。

Conclusion: 建立了轨道选择性机制：预先存在的交变磁序（由d_yz局域磁矩产生）与电子关联协作为d_xz-巡游电子驱动结构金属-绝缘体转变，展示了磁性序与电子关联在结构相变中的协同作用。

Abstract: A metal-insulator transition (MIT) driven by bond order (BO) coupled with a secondary spin-density wave (SDW) is identified in CsCr$_2$S$_2$O. Such coupling is enabled as a result of the broken time-reversal symmetry due to the pre-existing C-type antiferromagnetic (C-AFM) order. First-principles calculations reveal an orbital-selective physics that Cr-$d_{yz}$ orbitals form local moments and establish the altermagnetic order, while the Cr-$d_{xz}$ orbitals remain metallic and hybridize with S-$p_z$. Thus the low-energy physics is governed by the Cr-$d_{xz}$ and S-$p_z$ orbitals. On-site interactions then enhance a secondary SDW ($s$SDW) instability of the itinerant $d_{xz}$ electrons, which couples to the Cr-$d_{xz}$-S-$p_z$ bonding order. The resulting coupled $s$SDW-BO simultaneously produces experimentally observed structural distortion, charge disproportionation, local Cr-moment modulation, and gap opening. Our results establish an orbital-selective mechanism upon which pre-existing altermagnetism and electronic correlations cooperate to drive a structural MIT.

</details>


### [106] [Field-Selected Topological Buffering in a Disordered Skyrmion Crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28458)
*Wenyu Su,Nvsen Ma,Chen Cheng,Hong-Gang Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模模拟三角晶格手性磁体中的随机DM相互作用，揭示了磁场如何选择斯格明子晶体的两种无序化途径：高场下存在"拓扑缓冲"区间（布拉格玻璃态→斯格明子玻璃态），低场下该窗口消失，表明磁场可作为调控晶体无序与拓扑电荷损失分离的控制旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 淬火无序可以破坏晶体有序性而不立即摧毁其拓扑纹理的拓扑性，但在斯格明子晶体中，这两个过程之间的关系尚不清楚。理解这一关系对于揭示拓扑纹理如何在结构无序介质中保持鲁棒性至关重要。

Method: 采用大规模模拟方法，研究具有随机DM相互作用的三角晶格手性磁体；通过有限尺寸标度、空间关联函数、缺陷统计和自旋自关联函数等多种工具来分析无序驱动的结构转变和拓扑重构。

Result: 高场下，全局平移相干性在弱无序尺度丧失，六重键取向序存活到更大的无序强度，而总拓扑电荷几乎锁定到更大的尺度，形成包含布拉格玻璃态和斯格明子玻璃态的拓扑缓冲区间。低场下，键取向无序与拓扑重构几乎重合，斯格明子玻璃窗口消失，缓冲区间收缩。

Conclusion: 磁场是控制晶体无序化与拓扑电荷损失分离的关键参数，拓扑缓冲机制解释了拓扑纹理为何能在结构无序介质中保持鲁棒性，为理解和调控拓扑材料的无序行为提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: Quenched disorder can disrupt crystalline order without immediately destroying the topology of its constituent textures, but the relation between these processes in skyrmion crystals remains unclear. Using large-scale simulations of a triangular-lattice chiral magnet with random DM interactions, we show that the magnetic field selects between two disordering routes. At high fields, global translational coherence is lost at a weak-disorder scale, while sixfold bond-orientational order survives to a larger disorder strength and the total topological charge remains nearly locked up to a substantially larger scale. The resulting interval defines a topological buffer containing a Bragg-glass- like skyrmion regime followed by a skyrmion-glass regime. Finite-size scaling, spatial correlations, defect statistics, and spin autocorrelations support their distinct structural and glassy character. At lower fields, bond-orientational disordering nearly coincides with topological reconstruction, eliminating the skyrmion-glass window and contracting the buffer. These results identify the magnetic field as a control knob for separating crystalline disordering from topological-charge loss and establish topological buffering as a mechanism by which topological textures can remain robust in structurally disordered media.

</details>


### [107] [From Exact Diagonalization to DMRG: A Complete Numerical Study of the Transverse-Field Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28471)
*Chandra Sekhar Prayaga*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文对一维横场Ising模型（TFIM）进行了系统的数值研究，从精确对角化（L=8,14,20）到DMRG计算（直至L=100），在统一的方法论下追踪基态纠缠结构。识别并解决了早期数据集中因键维收敛不足导致的伪不连续性伪影，并通过两种互补途径提取临界中心荷c_eff≈0.51-0.52，与精确二维Ising值c=1/2一致。文章兼具物理结果与方法论示范双重目的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在提供一个自洽、系统性的横场Ising模型有限尺寸数值研究范例，既验证精确对角化与DMRG在特定条件下的严格一致性，又通过解析并修正早期数据中的键维收敛伪影（伪不连续性），警示数值实践者注意收敛诊断的重要性，并以此干净的数据集精确提取临界中心荷。

Method: 研究采用统一方法论：在七个系统尺寸（L=8,14,20,以及DMRG扩展至L=100）上使用相同的场网格和相同的收敛诊断标准（每个数据点在键维χ=100和χ=200处独立运行，检查收敛）。通过精确对角化与DMRG交叉验证，识别并修复键维收敛伪影。中心荷提取采用两条互补路径：（1）链中段纠缠熵的领头阶对数有限尺寸标度；（2）Calabrese-Cardy公式对每个键位、每个系统尺寸的全同时拟合，并系统排除短程晶格修正。

Result: 结果表明：在精确对角化与DMRG适用的重叠区域，两者必须精确一致而非仅近似一致；解析了键维收敛伪影导致的伪不连续性；通过两条互补途径获得中心荷c_eff=0.51-0.52，符合精确二维Ising普适类c=1/2。文章还回顾了Suzuki-Trotter量子-经典对应关系，并讨论了Widom-Kadanoff标度假设从经典到量子问题的移植。

Conclusion: 本文以横场Ising模型为平台，示范了谨慎有限尺寸数值计算的方法论价值：统一收敛诊断、交叉检验、系统排除有限尺寸修正，能够获得高精度的临界标度指数。同时，量子-经典对应关系为纠缠熵的共形标度行为提供了理论支撑。该工作既是一篇物理结果论文，也是面向数值实践者的方法论教学案例。

Abstract: We present a self-contained numerical study of the one-dimensional transverse-field Ising model (TFIM), tracing its ground-state entanglement structure from exact diagonalization at small system size (L=8,14,20) through density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) calculations up to L=100. A single, consistent methodology - identical field grid, identical convergence diagnostic (independent runs at bond dimensions chi=100 and chi=200 at every point) - is used across all seven system sizes, and we show explicitly where exact diagonalization and DMRG must agree exactly rather than merely approximately. Along the way we document and resolve a bond-dimension convergence artifact that produced a spurious discontinuity in an earlier, less systematic dataset, as a worked cautionary example for practitioners. Using the resolved dataset we extract the central charge of the transition via two complementary routes - the leading logarithmic finite-size scaling of the mid-chain entropy, and the full Calabrese-Cardy formula applied to every bond of every system size simultaneously - obtaining c_eff to 0.51-0.52 as short-distance lattice corrections are systematically excluded, consistent with the exact two-dimensional Ising value c=1/2. We review the quantum-classical (Suzuki-Trotter) correspondence that underlies this agreement and discuss how the Widom-Kadanoff scaling hypothesis transplants from the classical to the quantum problem. The manuscript is intended as both a physics result and a worked methodological example of careful finite-size numerics.

</details>


### [108] [Graph Neural Network Force Fields for Spin Dynamics in Metallic Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28537)
*Ali Rayat,Yunhao Fan,Gia-Wei Chern*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）的磁性力场框架，直接从电子结构计算中学习有效的磁能泛函，实现对巡游电子磁性系统中自旋动力学的高效预测模拟，在多种磁序（共线、非共线、非共面）体系中均与直接电子模拟结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属磁性材料的自旋动力学由电子产生的相互作用支配，传统预测模拟需要在时间演化过程中反复求解底层电子结构问题，造成巨大的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出图神经网络（GNN）磁性力场框架，直接从电子计算中学习有效的磁能泛函；该方法在概念上与机器学习原子间势（interatomic potentials）类似，能够高效评估自旋力矩，同时捕捉巡游电子产生的非线性、空间扩展的相互作用。

Result: 在具有共线、非共线和非共面磁序的代表性金属磁性体系中进行基准测试，学习到的力场能准确复现电子生成的自旋力矩，非平衡自旋动力学与直接电子模拟结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 图神经网络是机器学习磁性力场的有力框架，为跨多个长度和时间尺度的非平衡磁性大规模预测模拟提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Metallic magnets exhibit complex spin dynamics governed by electronically generated interactions. Predictive simulations of such dynamics typically require repeated solutions of an underlying electronic problem throughout the time evolution, creating a major computational bottleneck. Here we introduce a graph neural network (GNN) magnetic force-field framework that learns the effective magnetic energy functional governing itinerant spin dynamics directly from electronic calculations. Conceptually analogous to machine-learned interatomic potentials, the proposed framework enables efficient evaluation of spin torques while capturing the nonlinear and spatially extended interactions generated by itinerant electrons. We benchmark the method on representative metallic magnetic systems exhibiting collinear, noncollinear, and noncoplanar magnetic order. The learned force fields accurately reproduce electronically generated spin torques and yield nonequilibrium spin dynamics in excellent agreement with direct electronic simulations. Our results establish graph neural networks as a powerful framework for machine-learned magnetic force fields, providing a pathway toward predictive large-scale simulations of nonequilibrium magnetism across multiple length and time scales.

</details>


### [109] [Lattice composite Fermi liquid with broken inversion symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28613)
*Pavel A. Nosov,Zhengyan Darius Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了破缺反演对称性的半填充Chern带中晶格复合费米液体的输运性质，发现反转对称性破缺使复合费米子的奇异动力学响应显现出来，包括零波矢处非解析的纵向光电阻率、有限波矢处的非互易输运和非解析霍尔电导，以及通过2k_F散射重整化增强Umklapp弛豫导致的非解析温度依赖DC电阻率。这些输运特征在连续量子霍尔系统中不存在，可在扭转MoTe₂和菱面体石墨烯中直接检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解晶格复合费米液体与连续量子霍尔系统中复合费米液体在输运行为上的本质区别，特别是反演对称性破缺如何暴露被常规朗道能级设置所隐藏的奇异动力学响应。

Method: 在破缺反转对称性的半填充Chern带中，对复合费米液体进行理论输运研究。分析零波矢和有限波矢两种情形下规范场涨落对光电阻率和霍尔电导的非解析贡献，考虑门屏蔽库仑相互作用的影响；同时讨论复合费米面处2k_F散射重整化对Umklapp弛豫的增强效应。

Result: 发现零波矢处反转对称性破缺使规范场涨落产生非解析纵向光电阻率Reρ^{xx}(ω)∼|ω|^{4/3}（门屏蔽库仑相互作用下）；有限波矢q处导致非互易输运和非解析的∼|q|依赖的霍尔电导，可通过表面声波传播探测；此外2k_F散射重整化增强Umklapp弛豫，产生非解析的温度依赖电阻率。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了晶格复合费米液体的独特输运签名，它们在连续量子霍尔对应物中不存在，可直接在已有零场复合费米液体证据的扭转MoTe₂和菱面体石墨烯实验中检验。

Abstract: We study transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids realized in half-filled Chern bands with broken inversion symmetry. We show that reduced crystalline symmetry exposes intrinsic singular dynamical responses of composite fermions that are otherwise hidden in the conventional Landau-level setting. At zero wave vector, inversion breaking allows gauge-field fluctuations to generate a non-analytic longitudinal optical resistivity, with $\operatorname{Re}ρ^{xx}(ω)\sim |ω|^{4/3}$ for gate-screened Coulomb interactions. At finite wave vector $\mathbf{q}$, inversion breaking leads to nonreciprocal transport and a non-analytic $\sim |\mathbf{q}|$ dependence of the Hall conductivity, both of which can be probed through surface acoustic wave propagation. We also discuss a distinct mechanism for singular DC transport in lattice composite Fermi liquids: renormalization of $2k_F$ scattering at the composite Fermi surface enhances Umklapp relaxation and can lead to a non-analytic temperature dependence of the resistivity. Taken together, our results identify transport signatures of lattice composite Fermi liquids that are absent in their continuum quantum Hall counterparts and can be directly tested in ongoing experiments on twisted MoTe$_2$ and rhombohedral graphene, where evidence for zero-field composite Fermi liquids has recently been reported.

</details>

### [110] [Estimating Treatment Effects for Depression in Longitudinal Therapy Switching Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27214)
*Xinyu Qin,Martin Katzman,Alexandria Greifenberger,Elssa Toumeh,Sachinthya Lokuge,Tia Sternat,Ruiheng Yu,Lu Wang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用纵向MDD临床试验数据，通过公式化"下次复诊反事实预测任务"来估计不同换药方案下的HAMD-17量表总分，并在8种估计器中基准测试发现因果森林（CF）表现最稳定，识别出症状获益集中在特定换药方向且剂量强化总体有利，但存在特定患者亚组中低强度方案优于高强度的反直觉例外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症治疗常因疗效不足或不良反应需要换药，但个体化治疗的估计面临三大挑战：治疗分配受患者特征混淆、换药引入时变选择偏差、随访数据中无法观测反事实结局。现有方法难以准确估计此类场景下的个体化治疗效果，需要系统性地评估和基准测试不同的估计器。

Method: 基于专有的纵向重度抑郁症（MDD）临床试验数据集，构建下次复诊反事实预测任务来估计替代治疗方案下的HAMD-17总评分；基准测试8种估计器，包括元学习器（meta-learners）、基于残差的方法和基于树的方法，通过去混淆调整对治疗效果的估计进行校正。

Result: 因果森林（Causal Forest, CF）在所有评估标准下均表现最优且最一致。症状获益集中在特定的换药方向上，剂量强化总体上是有益的；但发现一个反直觉的例外情况——对特定患者亚组而言，低强度方案反而优于高强度替代方案。原始观察性比较显著高估获益，而去混淆调整后的估计值呈现出适中且可操作的效应量。

Conclusion: 该方法为抑郁症诊疗中的临床决策支持提供了可前瞻性检验的候选方案。调整混淆后的治疗效果估计具有实际临床指导价值，为抑郁症治疗中换药决策提供了数据驱动的个体化支撑。

Abstract: Depression treatment often requires switching medications due to inadequate response or adverse effects. Estimating individualized treatment effects in this setting is challenging because treatment assignment is confounded by patient characteristics, switching induces time-varying selection, and counterfactual outcomes are not observed in follow-up data. Using a proprietary longitudinal major depressive disorder (MDD) clinical trial dataset, we formulate a next-visit counterfactual prediction task to estimate Hamilton Depression Rating Scale (HAMD-17) total scores under alternative treatments. We benchmark 8 estimators, including meta-learners, residual-based methods, and tree-based approaches. Causal Forest (CF) demonstrates the most favorable and consistent performance across all criteria. Our analysis shows that symptom benefits concentrate in specific switch directions, with dose intensification being generally beneficial. Notably, we identify a counterintuitive exception where a lower-intensity regimen outperforms a higher-intensity alternative for specific patient subsets. While crude observational comparisons substantially overstate gains, confounding-adjusted estimates yield modest, actionable magnitudes. These findings provide prospectively testable candidates for clinical decision support in depression care.

</details>


### [111] [Foundation-Model Earth Representations Enable Regional-Scale Forest Aboveground Biomass Monitoring Across the Northeastern United States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27217)
*Shashika Lamahewage,Chandi Witharana*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用Google卫星嵌入（GSE，由AplphaEarth基础模型生成）结合机载LiDAR和连续森林清查数据，在美国东北部温带森林中实现区域尺度地上生物量（AGB）估算，R²达0.82，模型偏差降低70%以上，证明基础模型地球表征可支持规模化年度碳监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域尺度碳监测受限于野外清查和机载结构测量的时空稀疏性，缺乏可扩展的生物量监测途径。亟需利用全球一致性的地球观测基础模型表征来实现规模化、高频次的森林生物量估算。

Method: 将年度GSE观测、机载LiDAR和NEFIN连续森林清查数据集成到机器学习框架中；利用年度GSE观测通过时间生长调整将训练数据集扩展十倍以上；采用空间自相关分析评估残差空间依赖；通过蒙特卡洛模拟评估超参数优化对模型性能变异性的影响。

Result: LiDAR-GSE联合模型AGB估算R²为0.79；利用年度GSE观测扩展训练数据后R²提升至0.82，模型偏差降低超过70%；集成基础模型表征与结构预测因子显著降低了残差空间依赖性；超参数优化使模型性能变异性降低27.9%。

Conclusion: 基础模型地球表征捕捉了与森林生物量相关的生态学意义信息，为机载LiDAR覆盖不完整的地区提供了可扩展的年度碳监测框架，建立了基于全球可用基础模型地球观测的下一代森林碳评估途径。

Abstract: Forest aboveground biomass (AGB) is a critical indicator of ecosystem productivity and terrestrial carbon storage, yet regional carbon monitoring remains constrained by the sparse spatial and temporal availability of field inventories and airborne structural measurements. Recent Earth observation foundation models provide globally consistent geospatial representations derived from diverse multimodal datasets, offering a potential pathway toward scalable biomass monitoring. Here, we evaluate Google Satellite Embeddings (GSE), generated by the AplphaEarth Foundation Model, for regional-scale AGB estimation across diverse temperate forest ecosystems in the northeastern United States. We integrated annual GSE observations, airborne LiDAR, and continuous forest inventory measurements from the Northeastern Forest Inventory Network (NEFIN) within a machine-learning framework. Combined LiDAR-GSE models achieved an R^2 of 0.79 for AGB estimation. Capitalizing on annual GSE observations expanded the training dataset by more than tenfold through temporal growth adjustment, increasing predictive performance to R^2 = 0.82 while reducing model bias by over 70%. Spatial autocorrelation analyses showed that integrating foundation-model representations and structural predictors substantially reduced residual spatial dependence. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrated that hyperparameter optimization reduced model-performance variability by 27.9%. Our findings demonstrate that foundation-model Earth representations capture ecologically meaningful information relevant to forest biomass and provide a scalable framework for annual carbon monitoring in regions with incomplete airborne LiDAR coverage. Our fundings establish a pathway toward next-generation forest carbon assessment based on globally available foundation-model Earth observations.

</details>


### [112] [Psych-ECA: A Reproducible Semi-Synthetic Benchmark for Synthetic Control Arms in Longitudinal Psychiatry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27224)
*Aakash Bhagat,Shashank Choudhary*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出了Psych-ECA，一个针对精神科药物研发中外部/合成对照臂（ECA）评估的半合成基准生成器，模拟抑郁症（PHQ-9）、焦虑（HAM-A）和精神分裂症（PANSS）的纵向症状轨迹，具有已知反事实对照组、非随机访问时间和真实测量噪声。通过基准测试八种估计器，发现轨迹类方法和Scribe方法在准确性、校准性和可复现性方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神科药物研发正在引入外部和合成对照臂（ECAs），但该领域缺乏一个能评估监管机构所关心属性的基准：不仅包括方法重建未治疗轨迹的准确性，还包括不确定性是否经过校准、对精神健康记录中常见的信息性观察时间（病情较重的患者就诊更频繁）的鲁棒性，以及方法在go/no-go试验决策中引入的假阳性率。真实精神科试验数据（如STAR-D和注册队列）需要授权访问且缺乏真实反事实，因此需要可复现的半合成基准。

Method: 作者发布Psych-ECA，一个完全可复现的纵向症状轨迹生成器，涵盖PHQ-9、HAM-A和PANSS三个量表，具备已知反事实对照组、信息性访问和经过验证的尺度测量噪声。基准测试评估了八种估计器：携带前向填充、合并真实世界数据平均、最近邻匹配、线性混合模型、梯度提升和Scribe轨迹桥接方法。评估维度包括反事实准确性（RMSE）、预测区间校准性（经验覆盖率）以及在信息性采样下的鲁棒性（逆强度校正的偏倚减少和假阳性率）。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）轨迹类和灵活机器学习方法达到最佳反事实准确性（约2.3 PHQ-9 RMSE），优于横截面基线；（2）只有Scribe同时具备准确性和校准性，对90%名义预测区间达到93-96%的经验覆盖率，而梯度提升为87-88%，未校准的SDE模型仅62-75%；（3）逆强度校正减少了信息性采样下的偏倚，而Scribe的校准区间是唯一在信息性增加时维持名义假阳性率的轨迹方法。

Conclusion: Psych-ECA为精神科ECA方法评估提供了一个可复现的基准框架，填补了该领域在不确定性校准、信息性采样鲁棒性和决策假阳性率评估方面的空白。全部代码、数据生成脚本和随机种子均已公开，确保评估的完全可复现性。

Abstract: External and synthetic control arms (ECAs) are entering psychiatric drug development, but the field lacks a benchmark that evaluates the properties regulators care about: not only how accurately a method reconstructs untreated trajectories, but whether its uncertainty is calibrated, whether it is robust to the informative observation times common in mental-health records (sicker patients are seen more often), and what false-positive rate it induces in go/no-go trial decisions. Real psychiatric trial data (e.g. STAR-D and registry cohorts) require credentialed access and lack ground-truth counterfactuals, so, following established semi-synthetic benchmarks in causal inference (IHDP, ACIC, and the PK-PD tumor-growth simulator), we release Psych-ECA, a fully reproducible generator of longitudinal symptom trajectories for depression (PHQ-9), anxiety (HAM-A), and psychosis (PANSS) with known counterfactual control arms, informative visits, and validated-scale measurement noise. We benchmark eight estimators spanning carry-forward, pooled real-world-data averages, nearest-neighbour matching, linear mixed models, gradient boosting, and the Scribe trajectory-bridge method. Three findings emerge. First, trajectory and flexible machine learning methods achieve the best counterfactual accuracy (about 2.3 PHQ-9 RMSE), outperforming cross-sectional baselines. Second, only Scribe is both accurate and calibrated, achieving 93-96% empirical coverage of nominal 90% prediction intervals, compared with 87-88% for gradient boosting and 62-75% for uncalibrated SDE models. Third, inverse-intensity correction reduces bias under informative sampling, while Scribe's calibrated intervals are the only trajectory method that maintains nominal false-positive rates as informativeness increases. We release all code, data-generation scripts, and random seeds to enable fully reproducible evaluation.

</details>


### [113] [Who Moved? Ecological Estimates of Turnout & Coalition Support by Ethnicity and Age in Johor, Malaysia, 2022-2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27864)
*Thevesh Thevananthan,Ong Kian Ming*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文利用2022年以来柔佛州三次选举中投票站/投票流的人口结构差异，通过生态推断估计各族群与年龄层的投票率和联盟支持率，揭示了2026年国阵(BN)大胜背后的五个关键机制：投票率波动、马来选民从PN向BN的几乎一对一转移、年轻马来选民的大幅倒戈、华人选民因低投票率而流失、以及BN2026年表面的年龄梯度实为族群构成所致。该研究提供了首个开放且可完全复现的马来西亚选举生态推断估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2026年7月柔佛州选举中国阵(BN)大幅获胜，得票率较2022年几乎翻倍。作者希望理解这一剧烈变动背后的驱动因素，特别是族群和年龄维度上的投票行为变化，填补马来西亚选举研究中缺乏开放、可复现的生态推断估计的空白。

Method: 利用投票站层面的族群构成地理差异，以及行政管理造成的投票流内部年龄构成差异（Administration-induced variation），对自2022年以来涉及柔佛的三次选举进行生态推断（Ecological Inference），以估计按族群和年龄交叉分类的投票率与各联盟支持率。并通过held-out行为数据对每个估计量进行偏差验证。

Result: 五项关键发现：(1) 投票率是结果的首要驱动因素，同一族群内部跨选举变动幅度最高达26个百分点，且遵循生命周期曲线；(2) BN的激增几乎是马来选民中PN崩塌的一对一转移（在PN退出23/56议席的情况下依然稳健）；(3) 最大的BN回潮来自年轻马来选民——他们曾在2022年支持"绿潮"；(4) PH在华人选区的下滑中三分之二来自华人投票率下降，跨党转移比例相对较小；(5) BN在2026年显现的"年轻化"优势主要是族群构成所致而非族群内部行为差异——到2026年，各联盟在马来选民中的支持率已随年龄趋平。该研究提供了马来西亚选举中首个开放且可复现的生态推断估计。

Conclusion: BN在2026年柔佛州选的压倒性胜利是多重机制叠加的结果：马来选民（尤其年轻马来选民）的大规模倒戈、PN支持率的崩塌、华人选民投票率下滑以及族群构成效应共同塑造了选举格局。本文的方法论贡献在于提供了首个开放、可完全复现且有验证误差的马来西亚选举生态推断框架，为后续研究树立了范本。

Abstract: At the July 2026 Johor state election, Barisan Nasional (BN) swept the state and nearly doubled its vote relative to 2022. We exploit geographic variation in the ethnic composition of polling stations and administration-induced variation in the age composition of polling streams to estimate turnout and coalition support by ethnicity and age for the three elections involving Johor since 2022, with five key findings. (1) Turnout is a first-order driver of outcomes, moving by up to 26 points within an ethnic group across elections and following a life-cycle profile. (2) BN's surge was a near one-for-one Perikatan Nasional (PN) collapse among Malay voters, robust to PN's stand-down in 23 of 56 seats. (3) The largest reversal towards BN was among young Malays, who had previously carried the `green wave' of 2022. (4) Two-thirds of Pakatan Harapan's (PH) decline among the Chinese electorate came through lower Chinese turnout; any crossover to BN was comparatively small. (5) The apparent youth gradient in BN's 2026 support is largely ethnic composition rather than within-group behaviour: by 2026, Malay support for every coalition was close to flat in age. More broadly, we provide what we believe are the first open and fully reproducible ecological estimates of voting behaviour for any Malaysian election, accompanied by estimand-specific validation errors measured against held-out behaviour.

</details>


### [114] [What exam scores can and cannot prove about unauthorized AI assistance: Evidence from a highly public classroom episode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27978)
*Chad M. Topaz,Utsav Bahl*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究布朗大学一次期中考试作弊争议案例：期中成绩平均95.7分，期末考试改为监考后平均骤降至48.8分。通过排列检验和模型比较，发现学生两次成绩的相关性仅为0.06，无法检测到统计学上显著的关联，说明数据不支持期中成绩能预测期末成绩的假设。模拟实验表明，若学生成绩由其自身能力驱动，这两个模式几乎不会同时出现；只有当期中成绩与学生自身能力脱钩（如使用生成式AI）时才会出现。但论文也承认无法排除其他解释（如期末考不同内容），并提出了评估AI作弊统计证据的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在学术评估中的未授权使用已成为重大争议。当考试成绩出现异常下降时，如何用严谨的统计方法区分"作弊"与其他替代解释（如考试焦虑、试题难度、学生退课、均值回归）？作者希望通过真实案例数据，提供一种可复现的统计证据评估框架，帮助教育者在争议中做出基于数据的判断。

Method: 对59名学生的期中-期末成对成绩进行分析：(1) 计算两分数间的相关性和个体变化的分布模式；(2) 使用排列检验（permutation tests）检验期中与期末分数之间的统计关联，并评估不同替代解释的预测强度被检测到的概率；(3) 通过模型复杂度调整，比较"期中成绩不携带期末信息"的模型与"允许关联"的模型；(4) 进行模拟实验，检验"学生两次成绩由自身能力关联"的假设下两个观察模式（低相关性和巨大分数变化范围）是否会出现。

Result: 学生两次成绩相关性仅为0.06，个体变化范围从+4分到-100分。排列检验发现统计学上检测不到期中与期末分数之间的显著关联，而替代解释所预测的关联强度有超过90%的概率会被检测到。在考虑模型复杂度后，"期中成绩不携带期末信息"的模型与允许关联的模型拟合效果相当。模拟实验表明，在"两次成绩由学生自身能力相连"的假设下，两个观察模式几乎不会同时出现；只有在该关联被几乎切断时才会规律性地出现。

Conclusion: 数据不能排除作弊假设，但也不能唯一确证它——期中成绩被切断与期末联系的可能机制包括AI作弊（期中答案非学生本人完成）或期末考不同内容（无作弊）等。该研究不能判定哪些学生使用了未授权帮助，但提供了一个评估生成式AI在学术评估争议中统计证据的通用分析框架，呼吁以严格统计方法替代主观判断。

Abstract: In spring 2026, an economics professor at Brown University gave a take-home midterm and, after unusually high scores, made the final exam proctored. Among the 59 students who completed the course, average scores fell from 95.7 out of 100 to 48.8. The instructor attributed the drop to unauthorized use of generative AI on the midterm; others proposed test anxiety, a harder final, student withdrawals, and regression to the mean. The publicly released scores, one midterm-final pair per student, show two striking patterns: the correlation between a student's two scores is only 0.06, and individual changes range from a 4-point gain to a 100-point loss. Permutation tests find no statistically detectable association between students' midterm and final scores, yet would detect association of the strength the alternative explanations predict more than 90 percent of the time. Once model complexity is accounted for, a model in which a student's midterm carries no information about that student's final describes the data as well as any model that permits an association. Neither result rules out a weak association, but together they show that the data do not require any. In simulated classes where each student's two scores remain linked through that student's own proficiency, as the alternative explanations imply, the two patterns almost never appear together; they appear together regularly only when that link is nearly severed. More than one mechanism could have severed it: midterm answers that were not the students' own work would have done so, and so would a final testing substantially different material, with no assistance involved. The scores cannot distinguish these possibilities or identify which students, if any, used unauthorized assistance. Our study offers a framework for evaluating statistical evidence in future disputes over generative AI use in academic assessment.

</details>


### [115] [Bridging Probabilistic LLMs and Deterministic Statistical Validation: The PROVE Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical Trial Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28218)
*Zhaohua Lu,Cheng Zheng,Yuanyuan Han*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PROVE（Programmatic Reporting and Output Verification Engine），一个用于临床试验TFL（表格、图表和列表）自动化验证的可审计框架。PROVE采用可选的LLM和检索支持进行表格语义解释，但将数值和逻辑判定保留给程序化验证器。在十个复制的肿瘤学报告包评估中，PROVE在标签精确匹配场景下达到完美分类，在标签语义变体场景下LLM辅助匹配将整体F1从0.735提升至0.996，表明LLM最适合用于解释真实世界的TFL措辞变化，而最终数值验证仍应由可执行检查负责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床试验TFL的准确性和一致性验证在监管报告中仍是重大挑战。现有的独立编程和人工审查虽为必要的质量控制实践，但跨输出验证过度依赖审阅者的检查，可能遗漏结构、逻辑或算术方面的差异。虽然LLM能帮助解释多变的表格语言和导航冗长的研究文档，但它们不能可靠替代编程化的统计检查，因此需要一个将LLM的语义理解能力与程序化验证的可靠性相结合的框架。

Method: PROVE框架采用：(1) 可选的LLM和检索支持用于表格解释，(2) 程序化验证器负责数值和逻辑判定，(3) 将发现链接到源证据，(4) 支持跨输出一致性检查，(5) 允许根据研究需求启用或禁用LLM。评估设计为十个复制的合成肿瘤学报告包（由原始数据通过SDTM、ADaM和TFL生成），每包配对包含干净版和注入15个随机差异的版本，并测试两种标签设置（精确匹配与语义相似但措辞不同的标签）。

Result: 在实现的规则类别内，所有自动化PROVE变体在精确标签设置下均达到完美分类。在标签变体设置中，LLM辅助语义匹配将整体召回率从0.588提升至0.993，整体F1从0.735提升至0.996，显著优于精确匹配、模糊词法和嵌入相似性变体。

Conclusion: LLM最适用于解释真实世界中TFL措辞和格式的变化，即可执行检查应保留对最终数值验证的责任。PROVE框架通过将LLM的灵活语义理解与程序化验证的确定性相结合，在保证验证可靠性的同时提高了对真实变异问题的适应能力。

Abstract: Ensuring the accuracy and consistency of clinical trial Tables, Figures, and Listings (TFLs) remains a major challenge in regulatory reporting. Independent programming and manual review are essential quality-control practices, but cross-output verification still depends heavily on reviewer inspection and may miss structural, logical, or arithmetic discrepancies. Large language models (LLMs) can help interpret varied table language and navigate lengthy study documents, but they are not reliable substitutes for programmed statistical checks. We introduce PROVE (Programmatic Reporting and Output Verification Engine), an auditable framework that uses optional LLM and retrieval support for table interpretation while reserving numerical and logical decisions for programmed validators. PROVE links findings to source evidence, supports cross-output consistency checks, and allows LLM use to be enabled or disabled based on study requirements. We evaluated PROVE using ten replicated synthetic oncology reporting packages generated from raw data through SDTM, ADaM, and TFL outputs, with paired clean and discrepancy-injected packages; each replicate included 15 randomly injected discrepancies. We examined two table-label settings: exact labels matching the validator vocabulary and labels with similar clinical meaning but different wording. Within the implemented rule classes, all automated PROVE variants achieved perfect classification in the exact-label setting. In the label-variation setting, LLM-assisted semantic matching improved overall recall from 0.588 to 0.993 and overall F1 from 0.735 to 0.996 compared with exact-match, fuzzy lexical, and embedding-similarity variants. These findings suggest that LLMs are most useful for interpreting real-world variation in TFL wording and formatting, while executable checks should remain responsible for final numerical validation.

</details>


### [116] [Snapshot plots: displaying summary tables as parallel univariate plots with consistent color highlighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28302)
*Matthias Schonlau,Sandra Huang,Tiancheng Yang*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出"快照图"（snapshot plots），作为传统"Table 1"的可视化替代方案，用于展示研究样本的背景特征及分组差异。该方法基于hammock图的边缘情况，提供平行单变量图与一致性高亮，并附有网页应用和Python实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会科学和健康科学领域的实证研究中，"Table 1"通常用于展示样本背景特征并按治疗/对照组进行汇总统计。然而，传统表格在促进不同组间背景特征的比较方面存在不足，且对数值变量的细节呈现有限。因此需要一种更直观、信息更丰富的可视化方法来替代或补充这种表格。

Method: 提出快照图（snapshot plots），即带有一致性高亮的平行单变量图，用于可视化"Table 1"中的汇总统计。该方法作为hammock图（面向混合类别/数值型数据的平行坐标图）的边缘情况推导而来，并提供网页应用和Python实现。

Result: 通过两个"Table 1"实例展示了快照图的有效性，证明其能更好地促进不同组间背景特征的比较，并提供比传统表格更丰富的数值变量细节。

Conclusion: 快照图是"Table 1"的一种有效可视化替代方案，尤其适用于实验或观察性研究中治疗/对照组背景特征的对比分析，有助于研究者更直观地识别组间差异。

Abstract: For empirical studies, social and health scientists give background characteristics of their sample and summarize them in the famous "Table 1". When treatment/ control groups are present, this table gives summary statistics by group to see whether the background characteristics differ by group. We propose snapshot plots --- parallel univariate plots with consistent highlighting --- to visualize such tables. Compared to "Table 1", such plots are designed to facilitate comparisons of background characteristics --- in particular among groups --- and give more detail on numerical variables. We provide a web app as well as a python implementation of snapshot plots. Snapshot plots arise as edge cases of hammock plots (parallel coordinate plots for mixed categorical/ numerical data). We demonstrate the usefulness of snapshot plots for two "Table 1"s.

</details>


### [117] [Stated, Realized, and Optimal Aiming Strategies for the Tennis Serve: Experimental Evidence from Collegiate Athletes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28500)
*Nathan Sandholtz,Ryan Hanson,Ron Hager,Stephanie Kovalchik,Gilbert Fellingham*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文通过实验研究网球发球的最优目标瞄准位置，发现罚球最优目标比第一发球更保守，且球员实际发球中心比其声称的目标更保守、更接近理论最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通比赛数据无法观测球员发球时的意图目标，因此难以估计球员的发球执行误差，进而难以从理论上确定发球的最优瞄准位置。作者希望通过实验数据弥补这一信息缺失。

Method: 邀请杨百翰大学男子网球队队员，让每位球员标出他们认为最优的发球目标并做标记，然后对每个目标反复发球并记录精确落点。利用贝叶斯两期马尔可夫决策过程（连续空间行动），结合球员特定发球分布（同时处理被网拦截的审查发球）和估计的得分奖励曲面，求解最优瞄准位置。

Result: 在每一个球员-区域组合中，估计的第二发球最优目标都比第一发球最优目标更保守，与既有理论一致。球员声称的目标通常比最优更激进，但其实际发球中心比这些目标更保守，且明显更接近估计的最优值。

Conclusion: 实验数据能有效估计球员发球执行误差并推导最优发球策略；球员的实际发球行为虽与其声称目标有偏差，但更接近理论最优，说明球员可能具有内在的经验性校准能力。

Abstract: We study where, within a chosen Wide or T region, a tennis player should aim a serve. Aiming near a service-box boundary makes a serve harder to return but raises the probability of a fault, creating a trade-off that depends on the player's execution error. Ordinary match data provide limited information about execution error because players' intended targets are unobserved. We therefore conducted an experiment with members of the Brigham Young University men's tennis team. Each player identified targets they believed to be optimal, which we marked on the court. Players then served repeatedly toward each target while we recorded precise bounce locations. From these data, we estimate player-specific serve distributions while accounting for serves censored by net contact, then combine them with an estimated point-win reward surface in a Bayesian, two-period Markov decision process with a continuous spatial action space. We find that the estimated optimal second-serve target is more conservative than its first-serve counterpart in every player-region combination, consistent with prior theory. Also, while players' stated targets were generally more aggressive than optimal, their realized serve centers were more conservative than those targets and markedly closer to the estimated optima.

</details>


### [118] [ABC methods for IoT Emitter Geolocalisation using LEO Satellite Doppler Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28585)
*B. Ristic,Y. Choi,D. Y. Kim,A. Hourani*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究了利用低地球轨道（LEO）卫星在一次观测窗口内采集的多普勒频率测量数据，对地面静止IoT无线电发射机进行被动定位的问题；由于低成本IoT设备的无线电发射受到多种复合测量误差源影响，似然函数无法以闭合形式求解，因此采用近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）方法解决，并验证了拒绝采样ABC和序贯蒙特卡洛ABC两种方法的统计与计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本IoT设备发射信号的被动定位问题具有重要应用价值，但测量误差来源复杂且相互叠加，导致似然函数解析形式不可解，传统贝叶斯推断方法难以直接适用，需要探索不需要显式似然函数的推断方法。

Method: 采用近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）框架进行被动定位；具体研究了两种ABC实现方法：拒绝采样ABC和序贯蒙特卡洛ABC（SMC-ABC）方法，利用LEO卫星在观测窗口内采集的多普勒频率测量数据进行推断。

Result: 数值实验展示了两种ABC方法（拒绝采样ABC和序贯蒙特卡洛ABC）在被动定位任务中的统计性能和计算性能表现。

Conclusion: ABC方法为LEO卫星多普勒数据下的IoT发射机被动定位提供了一条可行路径，能够在似然函数不可解析处理的条件下实现有效的统计推断；两种ABC方法的性能比较可为实际应用中的算法选择提供指导。

Abstract: We address the problem of passive localisation of a stationary, ground-level IoT radio emitter using Doppler frequency measurements collected by low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellites during an observation window. The problem is challenging because radio emission from low-cost IoT devices is affected by various compounding sources of measurement error, that collectively render the likelihood function intractable in a closed form. Hence, we apply and investigate the performance of Approximate Bayesian Computation (ABC) methods for this task. Numerical results demonstrate the statistical and computational performance of two ABC methods, rejection sampling ABC and sequential Monte Carlo ABC.

</details>

### [119] [Clifford-Appell formulation of a Dirac-type Kronig-Penney model in condensed matter physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27920)
*L. Aceto,P. A. Grassi,H. Malonek*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在(N+1)维Clifford分析框架下研究广义Kronig-Penney模型的稳态Dirac方程。通过引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子将自由Dirac系统重构为两个Clifford值场的耦合一阶系统，再利用特征超复变量将其解耦为两个广义Helmholtz方程。作者构造了适配的二元Clifford-Appell多项式基，将微分问题转化为代数递推关系，最终得到解的显式级数表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义Kronig-Penney模型是研究周期势场中粒子行为的经典量子力学模型，而高维情形下的Dirac方程求解仍缺乏统一的Clifford解析框架。作者旨在建立一个统一的理论框架，将自由传播问题、广义Helmholtz方程与Appell多项式系统在超复函数论中联系起来，从而为高维奇异势散射问题提供显式解。

Method: 1) 引入广义Cauchy-Riemann算子，将自由Dirac系统重构为两个Clifford值场耦合的一阶系统；2) 通过特征超复变量将耦合系统解耦为一对独立的广义Helmholtz方程；3) 构造适应广义Cauchy-Riemann算子的二元Clifford-Appell多项式基；4) 利用Appell展开将微分方程问题转化为展开系数的代数递推关系，得到解的显式级数表示。

Result: 成功获得了广义Kronig-Penney模型稳态Dirac方程在相互作用位点之外的显式级数解，建立了一个统一的Clifford解析公式化框架，明确揭示了广义Helmholtz方程与超复函数论中Appell多项式系统之间的显式联系。

Conclusion: 该工作为高维Dirac方程在周期势中的求解提供了新的Clifford分析工具，将经典Kronig-Penney问题推广到(N+1)维超复框架，并通过Appell多项式级数方法实现了从微分方程到代数问题的转化，为后续研究量子散射和能带结构奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: We investigate the stationary Dirac equation associated with a generalized Kronig-Penney model in (N+1)-dimensional Clifford analysis. Away from the interaction sites, the free Dirac system is reformulated by introducing suitable generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators, leading to a coupled first-order system for two Clifford-valued fields. By means of characteristic hypercomplex variables, this system is reduced to a pair of decoupled generalized Helmholtz equations. To construct explicit solutions, we introduce a bivariate Clifford-Appell polynomial basis adapted to the generalized Cauchy-Riemann operators. The Appell expansions transform the differential problem into an algebraic recurrence for the expansion coefficients, yielding explicit series representations of the solutions. The proposed framework provides a unified Clifford-analytic formulation of the free propagation problem and establishes an explicit connection between generalized Helmholtz equations, and Appell polynomial systems in hypercomplex function theory.

</details>


### [120] [Consistent symmetry breaking and topological phases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28181)
*Yuezhao Li,Guo Chuan Thiang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了一个关于等变指标的规范弱/强二分法（canonical weak/strong dichotomy），该原理基于对称群不变算子在其任意有限指标子群下仍保持不变这一事实，要求等变指标在对称性破缺下保持一致，并研究了与粗几何（宏观）指标之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称群不变的算子在有限指标子群下仍然不变，因此这些算子的等变指标在对称性破缺（即从大对称群约化到子群）时必须保持一致性。然而目前缺乏一个通用的框架来系统性地建立这种一致性原则，本文旨在填补这一空白，并借鉴凝聚态物理中拓扑绝缘体的思想来构建该理论。

Method: 本文采用了一个源于固态物理中拓扑绝缘体理论的核心思想，即利用对称性破缺下等变指标必须保持一致性的原则，建立等变指标的规范弱/强二分法。同时，论文还探讨了这一框架与粗几何（coarse-geometric）或宏观指标（macroscopic indices）之间的内在联系。

Result: 成功建立了等变指标的规范弱/强二分法，证明了等变指标在对称性破缺下的一致性原则，并将该框架与粗几何指标的理论联系起来。

Conclusion: 等变指标理论可以通过对称性破缺的一致性原理获得一个规范化的弱/强分类结构，这一结构与粗几何（宏观）指标存在深刻关联，为理解拓扑不变量在对称性约化下的行为提供了统一视角。

Abstract: Operators invariant under a symmetry group are also invariant under any finite-index subgroup. The equivariant indices of such operators must be consistent under symmetry breaking. We use this principle to establish a canonical weak/strong dichotomy for equivariant indices, building on an idea originating in the theory of topological insulators in solid-state physics. We also study the relationship to coarse-geometric, or macroscopic, indices.

</details>


### [121] [The reduced Dirac structure of General Relativity on manifolds with corners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28262)
*Alberto S. Cattaneo,Filippo Fila-Robattino,Manuel Tecchiolli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了四维Palatini-Cartan引力的角点Poisson结构。基于带边界流形上引力的经典描述（即边界约束代数），在角点场空间上构造了一个预Dirac结构，并通过约化过程得到极大Dirac结构（即一个Poisson双矢场的图）。该Poisson结构被证明等价于一个仿射Poisson描述，由此将约化角点理论自然地呈现为BF类理论，并导出了BF²V表述，为统一描述Palatini-Cartan引力的体、边、角结构提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力理论中体（bulk）、边界（boundary）与角点（corner）结构的统一描述是一个重要而困难的问题。Palatini-Cartan引力作为一类重要的规范理论表述，其角点处（即边界与边界的交集）的辛/Poisson结构尚未被系统阐明。理解角点处的规范对称性代数及其Poisson结构，对于量子引力的协变相空间量化、边界荷与纠缠熵等议题具有重要意义。

Method: 论文采用以下方法：(1) 从带边界流形上Palatini-Cartan引力的经典描述出发，利用边界约束代数构造角点场空间；(2) 在角点场空间上构建预Dirac结构；(3) 通过约化程序得到极大Dirac结构，并将其等同于某个Poisson双矢场的图；(4) 进一步证明该Poisson结构可等价地表示为仿射Poisson结构，揭示其BF类理论的本质，并最终导出了BF²V（BF-squared-V）表述。

Result: 成功导出了四维Palatini-Cartan引力的角点Poisson结构，给出了从预Dirac结构到极大Dirac结构的约化构造；证明了该Poisson结构具有等价的仿射Poisson描述，使约化角点理论呈现为BF类理论，并建立了BF²V表述。

Conclusion: 本文为Palatini-Cartan引力的体、边界和角点结构提供了一个统一框架，为后续研究角点对称性、引力约束代数的几何结构以及量子化方案奠定了数学基础。

Abstract: In this paper, the corner Poisson structure of four-dimensional Palatini-Cartan gravity is derived. Building on the classical description of gravity on manifolds with boundary, specifically on the boundary constraint algebra, a pre- Dirac structure on the space of corner fields is obtained together with a reduction procedure that yields a maximal Dirac structure, identified as the graph of a Poisson bivector field, on the reduced space of corner fields. It is further shown that this Poisson structure admits an equivalent affine Poisson description, which naturally exhibits the reduced corner theory as a $BF$-like theory and leads to a BF$^2$V formulation. This provides the basis for a unified framework for the bulk, boundary, and corner structures of Palatini-Cartan gravity.

</details>


### [122] [Integrability in Asymptotic Symmetries of Spacetime: the $\text{BMS}_3$ scenario](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28454)
*Corentin Vitel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了Fuentealba等人于2018年提出的bms3可积层次，采用不同的结构方法（变分复形、Nijenhuis算子、τ-方案、Lie-Poisson描述及Lax流）系统地重构并拓展了该层次的理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在对先前提出的bms3可积层次提供更深刻且结构化的理解，通过多种严格数学工具（如变分复形、多哈密顿结构等）验证其内在一致性，并探索该层次的（非）唯一性及其与AdS3平坦极限和Lie-Poisson结构的联系。

Method: 1) 在多项式符号环上利用变分复形构造bms3双哈密顿结构，并附加Nijenhuis算子；2) 对AdS3情形进行类似构造，并检验其平坦极限是否恢复渐近平坦情形；3) 给出替代的τ-方案描述；4) 利用Lie-Poisson描述论证bms3层次的非唯一性；5) 对能量依赖的Schrödinger算子子类，证明其Lax流可由bms3的余伴随轨道刻画。

Result: 成功构造了bms3双哈密顿结构及其Nijenhuis算子；确认AdS3结构的平坦极限确实恢复渐近平坦情形；通过Lie-Poisson方法表明该可积层次并不唯一；并证明能量依赖Schrödinger算子的Lax流与bms3余伴随轨道存在精确对应。

Conclusion: 该工作从多个互补的结构视角（双哈密顿、几何、Lie-Poisson、Lax）系统性地重构并深化了对bms3可积层次的理解，揭示了其丰富的数学结构（如非唯一性与余伴随轨道联系），为3维渐近对称代数的可积系统理论提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We revisit the proof of a $\mathfrak{bms}_3-$integrable hierarchy introduced in Fuentealba et al. (JHEP, 2018), using different structural methodologies. Specifically, from the variational complex on the ring of polynomial symbols, a $\mathfrak{bms}_3$ bi-Hamiltonian structure is constructed on which a Nijenhuis operator can be attached. A similar argument is made for the $\mathrm{AdS}_3$ case, where we check that the flat limit of the latter recovers the asymptotically flat situation. An alternative $τ-$scheme description is also presented. Moreover, a Lie-Poisson description suggests that such a $\mathfrak{bms}_3-$hierarchy is not unique. For a subclass of so-called energy-dependent Schrödinger operators, it is shown that their Lax flows are described by the coadjoint orbits of $\mathfrak{bms}_3$.

</details>


### [123] [Redundant moments of non-melonic random tensor models: simplifying the tensor bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28518)
*Carlos I. Perez-Sanchez*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在任意张量模型中，所谓的"melonic族"大N矩是冗余的，任何melonic算子C在大N极限下的期望值仅通过其次数（degree）来依赖C。类似的结论也适用于melonic矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程。这一结果减少了张量bootstrap核心中正半定矩阵的独立条目数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 张量bootstrap是张量模型的一种广义化方法，其核心涉及正半定矩阵（类似于格点规范理论和随机矩阵bootstrap中的Toeplitz和Hankel矩阵）。减少这些矩阵中独立条目的数量可以显著简化bootstrap计算，提高数值效率。

Method: 通过数学证明的方式，展示了在大N极限下任何melonic算子C的期望值仅依赖于C的degree而非其具体结构，从而证明了melonic族的大N矩是冗余的。同时对melonic矩的Schwinger-Dyson方程也证明了类似的性质。

Result: 作者成功证明了melonic族大N矩的冗余性，即任意melonic算子C的期望值只通过degree依赖C。类似结论对Schwinger-Dyson方程同样成立。该结论的适用范围不限于melonic张量模型本身。

Conclusion: 该结果通过减少张量bootstrap中正半定矩阵的独立条目数量，提高了张量bootstrap的效率；且该结论具有广泛适用范围，不局限于melonic张量模型。

Abstract: We prove the redundancy of a family (the so-called melonic family) of large-$N$ moments in arbitrary tensor models. This reduces the number of independent entries in the positive semidefinite matrices that build the core of the tensor bootstrap (for tensor models there are already several generalizations of the positive semidefinite Toeplitz and Hankel matrices of lattice gauge theory and random matrix bootstrap, respectively). More concretely, we prove that any melonic operator $C$ at large-$N$ has an expectation value that depends on $C$ exclusively through its degree. A similar statement is shown here to hold for the Schwinger-Dyson equations of melonic moments. The strength of our result is also its scope, which is not limited to melonic tensor models.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Identifying common backbones of interactions underlying food webs via non-deterministic alignments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27496)
*Yifan Xu,Carlos Taveras,Lydia Beaudrot,César A. Uribe*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于最优传输理论（Gromov-Wasserstein）的可扩展非确定性网络对齐框架，用于识别跨生态系统中持久存在的食物网结构骨干。该方法支持多对多物种角色映射，并在129个撒哈拉以南非洲哺乳动物食物网上验证，发现了比零模型更具连通性和传递性的稳健骨干结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化通过改变物种分布和种间相互作用重塑食物网，因此识别跨生态系统持续存在的相互作用结构骨干至关重要。然而，现有的确定性对齐方法计算速度慢，且仅限于一对一的对应关系，无法捕获物种角色重叠的复杂场景。

Method: 提出一种受最优传输启发的可扩展非确定性对齐框架，通过基序角色配置文件（motif-role profiles）将网络对齐问题建模为Gromov-Wasserstein输运问题，实现高效且可解释的多对多物种角色映射。

Result: 将该方法应用于129个撒哈拉以南非洲哺乳动物食物网的大规模大陆尺度数据集。识别的成对对齐揭示了比零模型预期具有更高连通性和传递性的稳健骨干结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为预测生态系统重组和指导保护工作提供了形式化、可复现的工具，能够揭示跨生态系统持续存在的食物网相互作用结构。

Abstract: Climate change reshapes food webs by altering species distributions and interactions, making it essential to identify structural backbones of interactions that persist across ecosystems. Deterministic alignment methods are computationally slow and restricted to one-to-one correspondences. We introduce a scalable, non-deterministic alignment framework inspired by optimal transport that captures overlapping species roles via many-to-many mappings. Framed via motif-role profiles as a Gromov-Wasserstein transport problem, our method is both efficient and interpretable. We apply the proposed method to a large continental-scale data set of 129 mammal food webs in Sub-Saharan Africa. Pairwise alignments are identified, and we uncover robust backbones with greater connectivity and transitivity than those expected under null models. The proposed approach provides a formal, reproducible tool for forecasting ecosystem reorganization and conservation efforts.

</details>
